Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a reinforcement algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 732. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 535/2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the reinforcement space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 732 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 535. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
